colBegin).\n// The generator is a pure function of (N, i, j), so every rank can produce exactly\n// the part of the input it owns without any communication.\nvoid initMatrixBlock(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N, const size_t rowBegin,\n                     const size_t rowEnd, const size_t colBegin, const size_t colEnd) {\n    const size_t ld = colEnd - colBegin;\n    for (size_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {\n        double* row = mat.data() + (i - rowBegin) * ld;\n        for (size_t j = colBegin; j < colEnd; ++j) {\n            row[j - colBegin] = getPseudoRndValue(N, i, j);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Node-local GEMM: cache-blocked, packed, register-blocked kernel.\n// Computes C(m x N) += A(m x N) * B(N x N) for the locally owned row block.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nnamespace {\n\n// SIMD width (in doubles) of the target ISA, using portable GCC/Clang vector\n// extensions so that no external kernel library is required.\n#if defined(__AVX512F__)\nconstexpr size_t VW = 8;\n#elif defined(__AVX__)\nconstexpr size_t VW = 4;\n#elif defined(__SSE2__) || defined(__aarch64__)\nconstexpr size_t VW = 2;\n#else\nconstexpr size_t VW = 1;\n#endif\n\ntypedef double vec __attribute__((vector_size(VW * sizeof(double))));\n// Same type, but usable on unaligned addresses (the packed buffers and C).\ntypedef double vecu __attribute__((vector_size(VW * sizeof(double)), aligned(8)));\n\nconstexpr size_t MR = 6;       // micro-kernel rows\nconstexpr size_t NR = 2 * VW;  // micro-kernel columns (two vector registers wide)\nconstexpr size_t MC = 120;     // rows of A per L2 block (multiple of MR)\nconstexpr size_t KC = 256;     // depth per block\nconstexpr size_t NC = 512;     // columns of B per L3 block\n\n// Pack a kc x nc block of B (row-major, leading dimension ldb) into column\n// panels of width NR: panel p holds rows 0..kc-1, columns p*NR..p*NR+NR-1.\nvoid packB(const double* __restrict B, const size_t ldb, const size_t kc, const size_t nc,\n           double* __restrict Bp) {\n    const size_t panels = (nc + NR - 1) / NR;\n    for (size_t p = 0; p < panels; ++p) {\n        const size_t j0 = p * NR;\n        const size_t jn = (j0 + NR <= nc) ? NR : nc - j0;\n        double* __restrict dst = Bp + p * kc * NR;\n        for (size_t k = 0; k < kc; ++k) {\n            const double* __restrict src = B + k * ldb + j0;\n            for (size_t j = 0; j < jn; ++j) {\n                dst[k * NR + j] = src[j];\n            }\n            for (size_t j = jn; j < NR; ++j) {\n                dst[k * NR + j] = 0.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Pack an mc x kc block of A (row-major, leading dimension lda) into row panels\n// of height MR, stored so that the micro-kernel reads MR contiguous values per k.\nvoid packA(const double* __restrict A, const size_t lda, const size_t mc, const size_t kc,\n           double* __restrict Ap) {\n    const size_t panels = (mc + MR - 1) / MR;\n    for (size_t p = 0; p < panels; ++p) {\n        const size_t i0 = p * MR;\n        const size_t in = (i0 + MR <= mc) ? MR : mc - i0;\n        double* __restrict dst = Ap + p * kc * MR;\n        for (size_t k = 0; k < kc; ++k) {\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < in; ++i) {\n                dst[k * MR + i] = A[(i0 + i) * lda + k];\n            }\n            for (size_t i = in; i < MR; ++i) {\n                dst[k * MR + i] = 0.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// MR x NR micro-kernel: acc = Ap_panel * Bp_panel accumulated over kc steps.\n// Written with explicit vector types so that the 2*MR accumulators stay in\n// vector registers for the whole k loop (2 loads + MR broadcasts + 2*MR FMAs\n// per step).\ninline void microKernelAcc(const double* __restrict Ap, const double* __restrict Bp,\n                           const size_t kc, vec (&acc)[MR][2]) {\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < MR; ++i) {\n        acc[i][0] = vec{};\n        acc[i][1] = vec{};\n    }\n\n    for (size_t k = 0; k < kc; ++k) {\n        const double* __restrict a = Ap + k * MR;\n        const vecu* __restrict b = reinterpret_cast<const vecu*>(Bp + k * NR);\n        const vec b0 = b[0];\n        const vec b1 = b[1];\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < MR; ++i) {\n            vec av;\n            for (size_t t = 0; t < VW; ++t) av[t] = a[i];\n            acc[i][0] += av * b0;\n            acc[i][1] += av * b1;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Full tile: accumulate straight into C.\nvoid microKernelFull(const double* __restrict Ap, const double* __restrict Bp, const size_t kc,\n                     double* __restrict C, const size_t ldc) {\n    vec acc[MR][2];\n    microKernelAcc(Ap, Bp, kc, acc);\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < MR; ++i) {\n        vecu* __restrict c = reinterpret_cast<vecu*>(C + i * ldc);\n        c[0] += acc[i][0];\n        c[1] += acc[i][1];\n    }\n}\n\n// Edge tile: write back through a padded scratch tile.\nvoid microKernelEdge(const double* __restrict Ap, const double* __restrict Bp, const size_t kc,\n                     double* __restrict C, const size_t ldc, const size_t mn, const size_t nn) {\n    vec acc[MR][2];\n    microKernelAcc(Ap, Bp, kc, acc);\n\n    alignas(64) double tile[MR][NR];\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < MR; ++i) {\n        reinterpret_cast<vecu*>(tile[i])[0] = acc[i][0];\n        reinterpret_cast<vecu*>(tile[i])[1] = acc[i][1];\n    }\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < mn; ++i) {\n        double* __restrict c = C + i * ldc;\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < nn; ++j) c[j] += tile[i][j];\n    }\n}\n\n// Scratch buffers for the packed panels, allocated once per rank.\nstd::vector<double> Bpack;\nstd::vector<double> Apack;\n\n// C(m x n) += A(m x kTotal) * B(kTotal x n), all row-major with the given\n// leading dimensions. C is accumulated into, never cleared.\nvoid localGemm(const double* __restrict A, const size_t lda, const double* __restrict B,\n               const size_t ldb, double* __restrict C, const size_t ldc, const size_t m,\n               const size_t n, const size_t kTotal) {\n    if (m == 0 || n == 0 || kTotal == 0) return;\n\n    if (Bpack.empty()) {\n        Bpack.resize(KC * ((NC + NR - 1) / NR) * NR);\n        Apack.resize(((MC + MR - 1) / MR) * MR * KC);\n    }\n    std::vector<double>& Bp = Bpack;\n    std::vector<double>& Ap = Apack;\n\n    for (size_t jc = 0; jc < n; jc += NC) {\n        const size_t nc = std::min(NC, n - jc);\n        for (size_t kc0 = 0; kc0 < kTotal; kc0 += KC) {\n            const size_t kc = std::min(KC, kTotal - kc0);\n            packB(B + kc0 * ldb + jc, ldb, kc, nc, Bp.data());\n\n            for (size_t ic = 0; ic < m; ic += MC) {\n                const size_t mc = std::min(MC, m - ic);\n                packA(A + ic * lda + kc0, lda, mc, kc, Ap.data());\n\n                for (size_t ir = 0; ir < mc; ir += MR) {\n                    const size_t mn = std::min(MR, mc - ir);\n                    const double* aPanel = Ap.data() + (ir / MR) * kc * MR;\n                    for (size_t jr = 0; jr < nc; jr += NR) {\n                        const size_t nn = std::min(NR, nc - jr);\n                        const double* bPanel = Bp.data() + (jr / NR) * kc * NR;\n                        double* cTile = C + (ic + ir) * ldc + jc + jr;\n                        if (mn == MR && nn == NR) {\n                            microKernelFull(aPanel, bPanel, kc, cTile, ldc);\n                        } else {\n                            microKernelEdge(aPanel, bPanel, kc, cTile, ldc, mn, nn);\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n} // namespace\n\n// Simple validation: compute a few elements and compare.\n// Each rank checks the points that fall into the C block it owns; it holds the\n// full A rows and full B columns needed for those, so no communication is required.\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,\n                    const std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N,\n                    const size_t rowBegin, const size_t rowEnd,\n                    const size_t colBegin, const size_t colEnd) {\n    // Check a few random positions\n    constexpr size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};\n\n    const size_t localCols = colEnd - colBegin;\n\n    bool valid = true;\n    for (size_t pi = 0; pi < 5; ++pi) {\n        for (size_t pj = 0; pj < 5; ++pj) {\n            const size_t i = checkPoints[pi] % N;\n            const size_t j = checkPoints[pj] % N;\n\n            if (i < rowBegin || i >= rowEnd || j < colBegin || j >= colEnd) continue;\n            const size_t li = i - rowBegin;\n            const size_t lj = j - colBegin;\n\n            double expected = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {\n                expected += A[li * N + k] * B[k * localCols + lj];\n            }\n\n            const double actual = C[li * localCols + lj];\n            const double relError = std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));\n\n            if (relError > 1e-6) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at (%zu, %zu): expected %.10f, got %.10f (error: %.10e)\\n\",\n                       i, j, expected, actual, relError);\n                valid = false;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    return valid;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Matrix size N (computes NxN * NxN) (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);\n\n    int rank = 0, numRanks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &numRanks);\n\n    size_t N = 512;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            N = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (rank == 0) printUsage(argv[0]);\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Matrix Multiplication Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"Matrix size: %zu x %zu\\n\", N, N);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n        printf(\"MPI ranks: %d\\n\", numRanks);\n    }\n\n    // Row-block distribution of A, B and C.\n    std::vector<int> rowCounts(numRanks), rowOffsets(numRanks);\n    {\n        const size_t base = N / static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);\n        const size_t rem = N % static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);\n        size_t off = 0;\n        for (int r = 0; r < numRanks; ++r) {\n            const size_t cnt = base + (static_cast<size_t>(r) < rem ? 1 : 0);\n            rowCounts[r] = static_cast<int>(cnt);\n            rowOffsets[r] = static_cast<int>(off);\n            off += cnt;\n        }\n    }\n    const size_t rowBegin = static_cast<size_t>(rowOffsets[rank]);\n    const size_t localRows = static_cast<size_t>(rowCounts[rank]);\n    const size_t rowEnd = rowBegin + localRows;\n\n    // Allocate matrices. Every rank stores only its own row block of A, B and C,\n    // so the memory per rank scales as O(N^2 / P).\n    size_t maxRows = 0;\n    for (int r = 0; r < numRanks; ++r) maxRows = std::max(maxRows, static_cast<size_t>(rowCounts[r]));\n\n    std::vector<double> A(localRows * N);\n    std::vector<double> B(localRows * N);\n    std::vector<double> C(localRows * N, 0.0);\n\n    // Initialize matrices: the generator is a pure function of (N, i, j), so every\n    // rank can produce exactly the data it needs without any communication.\n    if (rank == 0) printf(\"Initializing matrices...\\n\");\n    initMatrixRows(A, N, rowBegin, rowEnd);\n    initMatrixRows(B, N, rowBegin, rowEnd);\n\n    // Ring-shift buffers for the B blocks travelling around the process ring.\n    std::vector<double> ringBuf[2];\n#ifndef REPLICATE_B\n    ringBuf[0].resize(maxRows * N);\n    ringBuf[1].resize(maxRows * N);\n#else\n    std::vector<double> Bfull(N * N);\n    {\n        std::vector<int> bc(numRanks), bd(numRanks);\n        for (int r = 0; r < numRanks; ++r) {\n            bc[r] = rowCounts[r] * static_cast<int>(N);\n            bd[r] = rowOffsets[r] * static_cast<int>(N);\n        }\n        MPI_Allgatherv(B.data(), bc[rank], MPI_DOUBLE, Bfull.data(), bc.data(), bd.data(),\n                       MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    }\n#endif\n\n    // Perform matrix multiplication\n    if (rank == 0) printf(\"Computing matrix multiplication...\\n\");\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n#ifdef REPLICATE_B\n    localGemm(A.data(), N, Bfull.data(), N, C.data(), N, localRows, N, N);\n#else\n    // C_local = sum_j A_local[:, block j] * B_block_j.\n    // The blocks are rotated through a ring of processes; the transfer of the next\n    // block overlaps with the multiplication using the current one.\n    {\n        const int nextRank = (rank + 1) % numRanks;          // we receive from here\n        const int prevRank = (rank - 1 + numRanks) % numRanks; // we send to here\n\n        std::copy(B.begin(), B.end(), ringBuf[0].begin());\n\n        for (int step = 0; step < numRanks; ++step) {\n            const int cur = (rank + step) % numRanks;   // block currently held\n            const int nxt = (rank + step + 1) % numRanks; // block arriving next\n            double* curBuf = ringBuf[step & 1].data();\n            double* nxtBuf = ringBuf[(step + 1) & 1].data();\n\n            MPI_Request reqs[2];\n            int numReqs = 0;\n            if (step + 1 < numRanks) {\n                MPI_Irecv(nxtBuf, rowCounts[nxt] * static_cast<int>(N), MPI_DOUBLE, nextRank, 0,\n                          MPI_COMM_WORLD, &reqs[numReqs++]);\n                MPI_Isend(curBuf, rowCounts[cur] * static_cast<int>(N), MPI_DOUBLE, prevRank, 0,\n                          MPI_COMM_WORLD, &reqs[numReqs++]);\n            }\n\n            localGemm(A.data() + rowOffsets[cur], N, curBuf, N, C.data(), N, localRows, N,\n                      static_cast<size_t>(rowCounts[cur]));\n\n            if (numReqs > 0) MPI_Waitall(numReqs, reqs, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);\n        }\n    }\n#endif\n\n    // Assemble the full result on rank 0.\n    std::vector<double> Cfull;\n    {\n        std::vector<int> counts(numRanks), displs(numRanks);\n        for (int r = 0; r < numRanks; ++r) {\n            counts[r] = static_cast<int>(rowCounts[r] * static_cast<int>(N));\n            displs[r] = static_cast<int>(rowOffsets[r] * static_cast<int>(N));\n        }\n        if (rank == 0) Cfull.resize(N * N);\n        MPI_Gatherv(C.data(), counts[rank], MPI_DOUBLE, Cfull.data(), counts.data(), displs.data(),\n                    MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    }\n\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    long long elapsedMs = duration.count();\n    MPI_Allreduce(MPI_IN_PLACE, &elapsedMs, 1, MPI_LONG_LONG, MPI_MAX, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Computation time: %lld ms\\n\", elapsedMs);\n\n        // Calculate GFLOPS\n        double gflops = (2.0 * N * N * N) / (elapsedMs / 1000.0) / 1e9;\n        printf(\"Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\\n\", gflops);\n    }\n\n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults && rank == 0) {\n        print_results(Cfull, \"MatrixC\");\n    }\n\n    // Validation\n    int rc = 0;\n    if (validate) {\n        if (rank == 0) printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n        int valid = validateResult(A, B, C, N, rowBegin, rowEnd, rowCounts, rowOffsets) ? 1 : 0;\n        MPI_Allreduce(MPI_IN_PLACE, &valid, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_LAND, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n        if (valid) {\n            if (rank == 0) printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            rc = 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            rc = 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    MPI_Finalize();\n    return rc;\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":294,"oldLines":109,"newStart":294,"newLines":55,"lines":["         printf(\"MPI ranks: %d\\n\", numRanks);","     }"," ","-    // Row-block distribution of A, B and C.","-    std::vector<int> rowCounts(numRanks), rowOffsets(numRanks);","-    {","-        const size_t base = N / static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);","-        const size_t rem = N % static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);","-        size_t off = 0;","-        for (int r = 0; r < numRanks; ++r) {","-            const size_t cnt = base + (static_cast<size_t>(r) < rem ? 1 : 0);","-            rowCounts[r] = static_cast<int>(cnt);","-            rowOffsets[r] = static_cast<int>(off);","-            off += cnt;","-        }","+    // 2D block decomposition of C over a gridRows x gridCols process grid, chosen","+    // as square as possible. i < rowEnd; ++i) {\n        double* row = mat.data() + (i - rowBegin) * ld;\n        for (size_t j = colBegin; j < colEnd; ++j) {\n            row[j - colBegin] = getPseudoRndValue(N, i, j);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Node-local GEMM: cache-blocked, packed, register-blocked kernel.\n// Computes C(m x N) += A(m x N) * B(N x N) for the locally owned row block.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nnamespace {\n\n// SIMD width (in doubles) of the target ISA, using portable GCC/Clang vector\n// extensions so that no external kernel library is required.\n#if defined(__AVX512F__)\nconstexpr size_t VW = 8;\n#elif defined(__AVX__)\nconstexpr size_t VW = 4;\n#elif defined(__SSE2__) || defined(__aarch64__)\nconstexpr size_t VW = 2;\n#else\nconstexpr size_t VW = 1;\n#endif\n\ntypedef double vec __attribute__((vector_size(VW * sizeof(double))));\n// Same type, but usable on unaligned addresses (the packed buffers and C).\ntypedef double vecu __attribute__((vector_size(VW * sizeof(double)), aligned(8)));\n\nconstexpr size_t MR = 6;       // micro-kernel rows\nconstexpr size_t NR = 2 * VW;  // micro-kernel columns (two vector registers wide)\nconstexpr size_t MC = 120;     // rows of A per L2 block (multiple of MR)\nconstexpr size_t KC = 256;     // depth per block\nconstexpr size_t NC = 512;     // columns of B per L3 block\n\n// Pack a kc x nc block of B (row-major, leading dimension ldb) into column\n// panels of width NR: panel p holds rows 0..kc-1, columns p*NR..p*NR+NR-1.\nvoid packB(const double* __restrict B, const size_t ldb, const size_t kc, const size_t nc,\n           double* __restrict Bp) {\n    const size_t panels = (nc + NR - 1) / NR;\n    for (size_t p = 0; p < panels; ++p) {\n        const size_t j0 = p * NR;\n        const size_t jn = (j0 + NR <= nc) ? NR : nc - j0;\n        double* __restrict dst = Bp + p * kc * NR;\n        for (size_t k = 0; k < kc; ++k) {\n            const double* __restrict src = B + k * ldb + j0;\n            for (size_t j = 0; j < jn; ++j) {\n                dst[k * NR + j] = src[j];\n            }\n            for (size_t j = jn; j < NR; ++j) {\n                dst[k * NR + j] = 0.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Pack an mc x kc block of A (row-major, leading dimension lda) into row panels\n// of height MR, stored so that the micro-kernel reads MR contiguous values per k.\nvoid packA(const double* __restrict A, const size_t lda, const size_t mc, const size_t kc,\n           double* __restrict Ap) {\n    const size_t panels = (mc + MR - 1) / MR;\n    for (size_t p = 0; p < panels; ++p) {\n        const size_t i0 = p * MR;\n        const size_t in = (i0 + MR <= mc) ? MR : mc - i0;\n        double* __restrict dst = Ap + p * kc * MR;\n        for (size_t k = 0; k < kc; ++k) {\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < in; ++i) {\n                dst[k * MR + i] = A[(i0 + i) * lda + k];\n            }\n            for (size_t i = in; i < MR; ++i) {\n                dst[k * MR + i] = 0.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// MR x NR micro-kernel: acc = Ap_panel * Bp_panel accumulated over kc steps.\n// Written with explicit vector types so that the 2*MR accumulators stay in\n// vector registers for the whole k loop (2 loads + MR broadcasts + 2*MR FMAs\n// per step).\ninline void microKernelAcc(const double* __restrict Ap, const double* __restrict Bp,\n                           const size_t kc, vec (&acc)[MR][2]) {\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < MR; ++i) {\n        acc[i][0] = vec{};\n        acc[i][1] = vec{};\n    }\n\n    for (size_t k = 0; k < kc; ++k) {\n        const double* __restrict a = Ap + k * MR;\n        const vecu* __restrict b = reinterpret_cast<const vecu*>(Bp + k * NR);\n        const vec b0 = b[0];\n        const vec b1 = b[1];\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < MR; ++i) {\n            vec av;\n            for (size_t t = 0; t < VW; ++t) av[t] = a[i];\n            acc[i][0] += av * b0;\n            acc[i][1] += av * b1;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Full tile: accumulate straight into C.\nvoid microKernelFull(const double* __restrict Ap, const double* __restrict Bp, const size_t kc,\n                     double* __restrict C, const size_t ldc) {\n    vec acc[MR][2];\n    microKernelAcc(Ap, Bp, kc, acc);\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < MR; ++i) {\n        vecu* __restrict c = reinterpret_cast<vecu*>(C + i * ldc);\n        c[0] += acc[i][0];\n        c[1] += acc[i][1];\n    }\n}\n\n// Edge tile: write back through a padded scratch tile.\nvoid microKernelEdge(const double* __restrict Ap, const double* __restrict Bp, const size_t kc,\n                     double* __restrict C, const size_t ldc, const size_t mn, const size_t nn) {\n    vec acc[MR][2];\n    microKernelAcc(Ap, Bp, kc, acc);\n\n    alignas(64) double tile[MR][NR];\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < MR; ++i) {\n        reinterpret_cast<vecu*>(tile[i])[0] = acc[i][0];\n        reinterpret_cast<vecu*>(tile[i])[1] = acc[i][1];\n    }\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < mn; ++i) {\n        double* __restrict c = C + i * ldc;\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < nn; ++j) c[j] += tile[i][j];\n    }\n}\n\n// Scratch buffers for the packed panels, allocated once per rank.\nstd::vector<double> Bpack;\nstd::vector<double> Apack;\n\n// C(m x n) += A(m x kTotal) * B(kTotal x n), all row-major with the given\n// leading dimensions. C is accumulated into, never cleared.\nvoid localGemm(const double* __restrict A, const size_t lda, const double* __restrict B,\n               const size_t ldb, double* __restrict C, const size_t ldc, const size_t m,\n               const size_t n, const size_t kTotal) {\n    if (m == 0 || n == 0 || kTotal == 0) return;\n\n    if (Bpack.empty()) {\n        Bpack.resize(KC * ((NC + NR - 1) / NR) * NR);\n        Apack.resize(((MC + MR - 1) / MR) * MR * KC);\n    }\n    std::vector<double>& Bp = Bpack;\n    std::vector<double>& Ap = Apack;\n\n    for (size_t jc = 0; jc < n; jc += NC) {\n        const size_t nc = std::min(NC, n - jc);\n        for (size_t kc0 = 0; kc0 < kTotal; kc0 += KC) {\n            const size_t kc = std::min(KC, kTotal - kc0);\n            packB(B + kc0 * ldb + jc, ldb, kc, nc, Bp.data());\n\n            for (size_t ic = 0; ic < m; ic += MC) {\n                const size_t mc = std::min(MC, m - ic);\n                packA(A + ic * lda + kc0, lda, mc, kc, Ap.data());\n\n                for (size_t ir = 0; ir < mc; ir += MR) {\n                    const size_t mn = std::min(MR, mc - ir);\n                    const double* aPanel = Ap.data() + (ir / MR) * kc * MR;\n                    for (size_t jr = 0; jr < nc; jr += NR) {\n                        const size_t nn = std::min(NR, nc - jr);\n                        const double* bPanel = Bp.data() + (jr / NR) * kc * NR;\n                        double* cTile = C + (ic + ir) * ldc + jc + jr;\n                        if (mn == MR && nn == NR) {\n                            microKernelFull(aPanel, bPanel, kc, cTile, ldc);\n                        } else {\n                            microKernelEdge(aPanel, bPanel, kc, cTile, ldc, mn, nn);\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n} // namespace\n\n// Simple validation: compute a few elements and compare.\n// Each rank checks the points that fall into the C block it owns; it holds the\n// full A rows and full B columns needed for those, so no communication is required.\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,\n                    const std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N,\n                    const size_t rowBegin, const size_t rowEnd,\n                    const size_t colBegin, const size_t colEnd) {\n    // Check a few random positions\n    constexpr size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};\n\n    const size_t localCols = colEnd - colBegin;\n\n    bool valid = true;\n    for (size_t pi = 0; pi < 5; ++pi) {\n        for (size_t pj = 0; pj < 5; ++pj) {\n            const size_t i = checkPoints[pi] % N;\n            const size_t j = checkPoints[pj] % N;\n\n            if (i < rowBegin || i >= rowEnd || j < colBegin || j >= colEnd) continue;\n            const size_t li = i - rowBegin;\n            const size_t lj = j - colBegin;\n\n            double expected = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {\n                expected += A[li * N + k] * B[k * localCols + lj];\n            }\n\n            const double actual = C[li * localCols + lj];\n            const double relError = std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));\n\n            if (relError > 1e-6) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at (%zu, %zu): expected %.10f, got %.10f (error: %.10e)\\n\",\n                       i, j, expected, actual, relError);\n                valid = false;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    return valid;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Matrix size N (computes NxN * NxN) (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);\n\n    int rank = 0, numRanks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &numRanks);\n\n    size_t N = 512;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            N = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (rank == 0) printUsage(argv[0]);\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Matrix Multiplication Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"Matrix size: %zu x %zu\\n\", N, N);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n        printf(\"MPI ranks: %d\\n\", numRanks);\n    }\n\n    // 2D block decomposition of C over a gridRows x gridCols process grid, chosen\n    // as square as possible. Rank r owns the C block of process row r / gridCols and\n    // process column r % gridCols, and correspondingly the A rows and B columns that\n    // block needs. This keeps the per-rank data volume at O(N^2 / sqrt(P)) instead of\n    // the O(N^2) that a pure row decomposition would stream through memory.\n    int gridCols = 1;\n    for (int c = 1; c * c <= numRanks; ++c) {\n        if (numRanks % c == 0) gridCols = c;\n    }\n    const int gridRows = numRanks / gridCols;\n    const int procRow = rank / gridCols;\n    const int procCol = rank % gridCols;\n\n    const auto blockBegin = [](const size_t total, const int parts, const int idx) {\n        const size_t base = total / static_cast<size_t>(parts);\n        const size_t rem = total % static_cast<size_t>(parts);\n        return base * static_cast<size_t>(idx) + std::min(static_cast<size_t>(idx), rem);\n    };\n\n    const size_t rowBegin = blockBegin(N, gridRows, procRow);\n    const size_t rowEnd = blockBegin(N, gridRows, procRow + 1);\n    const size_t colBegin = blockBegin(N, gridCols, procCol);\n    const size_t colEnd = blockBegin(N, gridCols, procCol + 1);\n    const size_t localRows = rowEnd - rowBegin;\n    const size_t localCols = colEnd - colBegin;\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"MPI ranks: %d (process grid %d x %d)\\n\", numRanks, gridRows, gridCols);\n    }\n\n    // Allocate matrices: the A row panel, the B column panel and the C block owned\n    // by this rank.\n    std::vector<double> A(localRows * N);\n    std::vector<double> B(N * localCols);\n    std::vector<double> C(localRows * localCols, 0.0);\n\n    // Initialize matrices\n    if (rank == 0) printf(\"Initializing matrices...\\n\");\n    initMatrixBlock(A, N, rowBegin, rowEnd, 0, N);\n    initMatrixBlock(B, N, 0, N, colBegin, colEnd);\n\n    // Perform matrix multiplication\n    if (rank == 0) printf(\"Computing matrix multiplication...\\n\");\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    localGemm(A.data(), N, B.data(), localCols, C.data(), localCols, localRows, localCols, N);\n\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - colBegin).\n// The generator is a pure function of (N, i, j), so every rank can produce exactly\n// the part of the input it owns without any communication.\nvoid initMatrixBlock(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N, const size_t rowBegin,\n                     const size_t rowEnd, const size_t colBegin, const size_t colEnd) {\n    const size_t ld = colEnd - colBegin;\n    for (size_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {\n        double* row = mat.data() + (i - rowBegin) * ld;\n        for (size_t j = colBegin; j < colEnd; ++j) {\n            row[j - colBegin] = getPseudoRndValue(N, i, j);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Node-local GEMM: cache-blocked, packed, register-blocked kernel.\n// Computes C(m x N) += A(m x N) * B(N x N) for the locally owned row block.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nnamespace {\n\n// SIMD width (in doubles) of the target ISA, using portable GCC/Clang vector\n// extensions so that no external kernel library is required.\n#if defined(__AVX512F__)\nconstexpr size_t VW = 8;\n#elif defined(__AVX__)\nconstexpr size_t VW = 4;\n#elif defined(__SSE2__) || defined(__aarch64__)\nconstexpr size_t VW = 2;\n#else\nconstexpr size_t VW = 1;\n#endif\n\ntypedef double vec __attribute__((vector_size(VW * sizeof(double))));\n// Same type, but usable on unaligned addresses (the packed buffers and C).\ntypedef double vecu __attribute__((vector_size(VW * sizeof(double)), aligned(8)));\n\nconstexpr size_t MR = 6;       // micro-kernel rows\nconstexpr size_t NR = 2 * VW;  // micro-kernel columns (two vector registers wide)\nconstexpr size_t MC = 120;     // rows of A per L2 block (multiple of MR)\nconstexpr size_t KC = 256;     // depth per block\nconstexpr size_t NC = 512;     // columns of B per L3 block\n\n// Pack a kc x nc block of B (row-major, leading dimension ldb) into column\n// panels of width NR: panel p holds rows 0..kc-1, columns p*NR..p*NR+NR-1.\nvoid packB(const double* __restrict B, const size_t ldb, const size_t kc, const size_t nc,\n           double* __restrict Bp) {\n    const size_t panels = (nc + NR - 1) / NR;\n    for (size_t p = 0; p < panels; ++p) {\n        const size_t j0 = p * NR;\n        const size_t jn = (j0 + NR <= nc) ? NR : nc - j0;\n        double* __restrict dst = Bp + p * kc * NR;\n        for (size_t k = 0; k < kc; ++k) {\n            const double* __restrict src = B + k * ldb + j0;\n            for (size_t j = 0; j < jn; ++j) {\n                dst[k * NR + j] = src[j];\n            }\n            for (size_t j = jn; j < NR; ++j) {\n                dst[k * NR + j] = 0.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Pack an mc x kc block of A (row-major, leading dimension lda) into row panels\n// of height MR, stored so that the micro-kernel reads MR contiguous values per k.\nvoid packA(const double* __restrict A, const size_t lda, const size_t mc, const size_t kc,\n           double* __restrict Ap) {\n    const size_t panels = (mc + MR - 1) / MR;\n    for (size_t p = 0; p < panels; ++p) {\n        const size_t i0 = p * MR;\n        const size_t in = (i0 + MR <= mc) ? MR : mc - i0;\n        double* __restrict dst = Ap + p * kc * MR;\n        for (size_t k = 0; k < kc; ++k) {\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < in; ++i) {\n                dst[k * MR + i] = A[(i0 + i) * lda + k];\n            }\n            for (size_t i = in; i < MR; ++i) {\n                dst[k * MR + i] = 0.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// MR x NR micro-kernel: acc = Ap_panel * Bp_panel accumulated over kc steps.\n// Written with explicit vector types so that the 2*MR accumulators stay in\n// vector registers for the whole k loop (2 loads + MR broadcasts + 2*MR FMAs\n// per step).\ninline void microKernelAcc(const double* __restrict Ap, const double* __restrict Bp,\n                           const size_t kc, vec (&acc)[MR][2]) {\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < MR; ++i) {\n        acc[i][0] = vec{};\n        acc[i][1] = vec{};\n    }\n\n    for (size_t k = 0; k < kc; ++k) {\n        const double* __restrict a = Ap + k * MR;\n        const vecu* __restrict b = reinterpret_cast<const vecu*>(Bp + k * NR);\n        const vec b0 = b[0];\n        const vec b1 = b[1];\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < MR; ++i) {\n            vec av;\n            for (size_t t = 0; t < VW; ++t) av[t] = a[i];\n            acc[i][0] += av * b0;\n            acc[i][1] += av * b1;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Full tile: accumulate straight into C.\nvoid microKernelFull(const double* __restrict Ap, const double* __restrict Bp, const size_t kc,\n                     double* __restrict C, const size_t ldc) {\n    vec acc[MR][2];\n    microKernelAcc(Ap, Bp, kc, acc);\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < MR; ++i) {\n        vecu* __restrict c = reinterpret_cast<vecu*>(C + i * ldc);\n        c[0] += acc[i][0];\n        c[1] += acc[i][1];\n    }\n}\n\n// Edge tile: write back through a padded scratch tile.\nvoid microKernelEdge(const double* __restrict Ap, const double* __restrict Bp, const size_t kc,\n                     double* __restrict C, const size_t ldc, const size_t mn, const size_t nn) {\n    vec acc[MR][2];\n    microKernelAcc(Ap, Bp, kc, acc);\n\n    alignas(64) double tile[MR][NR];\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < MR; ++i) {\n        reinterpret_cast<vecu*>(tile[i])[0] = acc[i][0];\n        reinterpret_cast<vecu*>(tile[i])[1] = acc[i][1];\n    }\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < mn; ++i) {\n        double* __restrict c = C + i * ldc;\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < nn; ++j) c[j] += tile[i][j];\n    }\n}\n\n// Scratch buffers for the packed panels, allocated once per rank.\nstd::vector<double> Bpack;\nstd::vector<double> Apack;\n\n// C(m x n) += A(m x kTotal) * B(kTotal x n), all row-major with the given\n// leading dimensions. C is accumulated into, never cleared.\nvoid localGemm(const double* __restrict A, const size_t lda, const double* __restrict B,\n               const size_t ldb, double* __restrict C, const size_t ldc, const size_t m,\n               const size_t n, const size_t kTotal) {\n    if (m == 0 || n == 0 || kTotal == 0) return;\n\n    if (Bpack.empty()) {\n        Bpack.resize(KC * ((NC + NR - 1) / NR) * NR);\n        Apack.resize(((MC + MR - 1) / MR) * MR * KC);\n    }\n    std::vector<double>& Bp = Bpack;\n    std::vector<double>& Ap = Apack;\n\n    for (size_t jc = 0; jc < n; jc += NC) {\n        const size_t nc = std::min(NC, n - jc);\n        for (size_t kc0 = 0; kc0 < kTotal; kc0 += KC) {\n            const size_t kc = std::min(KC, kTotal - kc0);\n            packB(B + kc0 * ldb + jc, ldb, kc, nc, Bp.data());\n\n            for (size_t ic = 0; ic < m; ic += MC) {\n                const size_t mc = std::min(MC, m - ic);\n                packA(A + ic * lda + kc0, lda, mc, kc, Ap.data());\n\n                for (size_t ir = 0; ir < mc; ir += MR) {\n                    const size_t mn = std::min(MR, mc - ir);\n                    const double* aPanel = Ap.data() + (ir / MR) * kc * MR;\n                    for (size_t jr = 0; jr < nc; jr += NR) {\n                        const size_t nn = std::min(NR, nc - jr);\n                        const double* bPanel = Bp.data() + (jr / NR) * kc * NR;\n                        double* cTile = C + (ic + ir) * ldc + jc + jr;\n                        if (mn == MR && nn == NR) {\n                            microKernelFull(aPanel, bPanel, kc, cTile, ldc);\n                        } else {\n                            microKernelEdge(aPanel, bPanel, kc, cTile, ldc, mn, nn);\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n} // namespace\n\n// Simple validation: compute a few elements and compare.\n// Each rank checks the points that fall into the C block it owns; it holds the\n// full A rows and full B columns needed for those, so no communication is required.\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,\n                    const std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N,\n                    const size_t rowBegin, const size_t rowEnd,\n                    const size_t colBegin, const size_t colEnd) {\n    // Check a few random positions\n    constexpr size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};\n\n    const size_t localCols = colEnd - colBegin;\n\n    bool valid = true;\n    for (size_t pi = 0; pi < 5; ++pi) {\n        for (size_t pj = 0; pj < 5; ++pj) {\n            const size_t i = checkPoints[pi] % N;\n            const size_t j = checkPoints[pj] % N;\n\n            if (i < rowBegin || i >= rowEnd || j < colBegin || j >= colEnd) continue;\n            const size_t li = i - rowBegin;\n            const size_t lj = j - colBegin;\n\n            double expected = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {\n                expected += A[li * N + k] * B[k * localCols + lj];\n            }\n\n            const double actual = C[li * localCols + lj];\n            const double relError = std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));\n\n            if (relError > 1e-6) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at (%zu, %zu): expected %.10f, got %.10f (error: %.10e)\\n\",\n                       i, j, expected, actual, relError);\n                valid = false;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    return valid;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Matrix size N (computes NxN * NxN) (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);\n\n    int rank = 0, numRanks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &numRanks);\n\n    size_t N = 512;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            N = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (rank == 0) printUsage(argv[0]);\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Matrix Multiplication Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"Matrix size: %zu x %zu\\n\", N, N);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    }\n\n    // 2D block decomposition of C over a gridRows x gridCols process grid, chosen\n    // as square as possible. Rank r owns the C block of process row r / gridCols and\n    // process column r % gridCols, and correspondingly the A rows and B columns that\n    // block needs. This keeps the per-rank data volume at O(N^2 / sqrt(P)) instead of\n    // the O(N^2) that a pure row decomposition would stream through memory.\n    int gridCols = 1;\n    for (int c = 1; c * c <= numRanks; ++c) {\n        if (numRanks % c == 0) gridCols = c;\n    }\n    const int gridRows = numRanks / gridCols;\n    const int procRow = rank / gridCols;\n    const int procCol = rank % gridCols;\n\n    const auto blockBegin = [](const size_t total, const int parts, const int idx) {\n        const size_t base = total / static_cast<size_t>(parts);\n        const size_t rem = total % static_cast<size_t>(parts);\n        return base * static_cast<size_t>(idx) + std::min(static_cast<size_t>(idx), rem);\n    };\n\n    const size_t rowBegin = blockBegin(N, gridRows, procRow);\n    const size_t rowEnd = blockBegin(N, gridRows, procRow + 1);\n    const size_t colBegin = blockBegin(N, gridCols, procCol);\n    const size_t colEnd = blockBegin(N, gridCols, procCol + 1);\n    const size_t localRows = rowEnd - rowBegin;\n    const size_t localCols = colEnd - colBegin;\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"MPI ranks: %d (process grid %d x %d)\\n\", numRanks, gridRows, gridCols);\n    }\n\n    // Allocate matrices: the A row panel, the B column panel and the C block owned\n    // by this rank.\n    std::vector<double> A(localRows * N);\n    std::vector<double> B(N * localCols);\n    std::vector<double> C(localRows * localCols, 0.0);\n\n    // Initialize matrices\n    if (rank == 0) printf(\"Initializing matrices...\\n\");\n    initMatrixBlock(A, N, rowBegin, rowEnd, 0, N);\n    initMatrixBlock(B, N, 0, N, colBegin, colEnd);\n\n    // Perform matrix multiplication\n    if (rank == 0) printf(\"Computing matrix multiplication...\\n\");\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    localGemm(A.data(), N, B.data(), localCols, C.data(), localCols, localRows, localCols, N);\n\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    long long elapsedMs = duration.count();\n    MPI_Allreduce(MPI_IN_PLACE, &elapsedMs, 1, MPI_LONG_LONG, MPI_MAX, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Computation time: %lld ms\\n\", elapsedMs);\n\n        // Calculate GFLOPS\n        double gflops = (2.0 * N * N * N) / (elapsedMs / 1000.0) / 1e9;\n        printf(\"Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\\n\", gflops);\n    }\n\n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults && rank == 0) {\n        print_results(Cfull, \"MatrixC\");\n    }\n\n    // Validation\n    int rc = 0;\n    if (validate) {\n        if (rank == 0) printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n        int valid = validateResult(A, B, C, N, rowBegin, rowEnd, rowCounts, rowOffsets) ? 1 : 0;\n        MPI_Allreduce(MPI_IN_PLACE, &valid, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_LAND, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n        if (valid) {\n            if (rank == 0) printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            rc = 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) i < rowEnd; ++i) {\n        double* row = mat.data() + (i - rowBegin) * ld;\n        for (size_t j = colBegin; j < colEnd; ++j) {\n            row[j - colBegin] = getPseudoRndValue(N, i, j);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Node-local GEMM: cache-blocked, packed, register-blocked kernel.\n// Computes C(m x N) += A(m x N) * B(N x N) for the locally owned row block.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nnamespace {\n\n// SIMD width (in doubles) of the target ISA, using portable GCC/Clang vector\n// extensions so that no external kernel library is required.\n#if defined(__AVX512F__)\nconstexpr size_t VW = 8;\n#elif defined(__AVX__)\nconstexpr size_t VW = 4;\n#elif defined(__SSE2__) || defined(__aarch64__)\nconstexpr size_t VW = 2;\n#else\nconstexpr size_t VW = 1;\n#endif\n\ntypedef double vec __attribute__((vector_size(VW * sizeof(double))));\n// Same type, but usable on unaligned addresses (the packed buffers and C).\ntypedef double vecu __attribute__((vector_size(VW * sizeof(double)), aligned(8)));\n\nconstexpr size_t MR = 6;       // micro-kernel rows\nconstexpr size_t NR = 2 * VW;  // micro-kernel columns (two vector registers wide)\nconstexpr size_t MC = 120;     // rows of A per L2 block (multiple of MR)\nconstexpr size_t KC = 256;     // depth per block\nconstexpr size_t NC = 512;     // columns of B per L3 block\n\n// Pack a kc x nc block of B (row-major, leading dimension ldb) into column\n// panels of width NR: panel p holds rows 0..kc-1, columns p*NR..p*NR+NR-1.\nvoid packB(const double* __restrict B, const size_t ldb, const size_t kc, const size_t nc,\n           double* __restrict Bp) {\n    const size_t panels = (nc + NR - 1) / NR;\n    for (size_t p = 0; p < panels; ++p) {\n        const size_t j0 = p * NR;\n        const size_t jn = (j0 + NR <= nc) ? NR : nc - j0;\n        double* __restrict dst = Bp + p * kc * NR;\n        for (size_t k = 0; k < kc; ++k) {\n            const double* __restrict src = B + k * ldb + j0;\n            for (size_t j = 0; j < jn; ++j) {\n                dst[k * NR + j] = src[j];\n            }\n            for (size_t j = jn; j < NR; ++j) {\n                dst[k * NR + j] = 0.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Pack an mc x kc block of A (row-major, leading dimension lda) into row panels\n// of height MR, stored so that the micro-kernel reads MR contiguous values per k.\nvoid packA(const double* __restrict A, const size_t lda, const size_t mc, const size_t kc,\n           double* __restrict Ap) {\n    const size_t panels = (mc + MR - 1) / MR;\n    for (size_t p = 0; p < panels; ++p) {\n        const size_t i0 = p * MR;\n        const size_t in = (i0 + MR <= mc) ? MR : mc - i0;\n        double* __restrict dst = Ap + p * kc * MR;\n        for (size_t k = 0; k < kc; ++k) {\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < in; ++i) {\n                dst[k * MR + i] = A[(i0 + i) * lda + k];\n            }\n            for (size_t i = in; i < MR; ++i) {\n                dst[k * MR + i] = 0.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// MR x NR micro-kernel: acc = Ap_panel * Bp_panel accumulated over kc steps.\n// Written with explicit vector types so that the 2*MR accumulators stay in\n// vector registers for the whole k loop (2 loads + MR broadcasts + 2*MR FMAs\n// per step).\ninline void microKernelAcc(const double* __restrict Ap, const double* __restrict Bp,\n                           const size_t kc, vec (&acc)[MR][2]) {\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < MR; ++i) {\n        acc[i][0] = vec{};\n        acc[i][1] = vec{};\n    }\n\n    for (size_t k = 0; k < kc; ++k) {\n        const double* __restrict a = Ap + k * MR;\n        const vecu* __restrict b = reinterpret_cast<const vecu*>(Bp + k * NR);\n        const vec b0 = b[0];\n        const vec b1 = b[1];\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < MR; ++i) {\n            vec av;\n            for (size_t t = 0; t < VW; ++t) av[t] = a[i];\n            acc[i][0] += av * b0;\n            acc[i][1] += av * b1;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Full tile: accumulate straight into C.\nvoid microKernelFull(const double* __restrict Ap, const double* __restrict Bp, const size_t kc,\n                     double* __restrict C, const size_t ldc) {\n    vec acc[MR][2];\n    microKernelAcc(Ap, Bp, kc, acc);\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < MR; ++i) {\n        vecu* __restrict c = reinterpret_cast<vecu*>(C + i * ldc);\n        c[0] += acc[i][0];\n        c[1] += acc[i][1];\n    }\n}\n\n// Edge tile: write back through a padded scratch tile.\nvoid microKernelEdge(const double* __restrict Ap, const double* __restrict Bp, const size_t kc,\n                     double* __restrict C, const size_t ldc, const size_t mn, const size_t nn) {\n    vec acc[MR][2];\n    microKernelAcc(Ap, Bp, kc, acc);\n\n    alignas(64) double tile[MR][NR];\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < MR; ++i) {\n        reinterpret_cast<vecu*>(tile[i])[0] = acc[i][0];\n        reinterpret_cast<vecu*>(tile[i])[1] = acc[i][1];\n    }\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < mn; ++i) {\n        double* __restrict c = C + i * ldc;\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < nn; ++j) c[j] += tile[i][j];\n    }\n}\n\n// Scratch buffers for the packed panels, allocated once per rank.\nstd::vector<double> Bpack;\nstd::vector<double> Apack;\n\n// C(m x n) += A(m x kTotal) * B(kTotal x n), all row-major with the given\n// leading dimensions. C is accumulated into, never cleared.\nvoid localGemm(const double* __restrict A, const size_t lda, const double* __restrict B,\n               const size_t ldb, double* __restrict C, const size_t ldc, const size_t m,\n               const size_t n, const size_t kTotal) {\n    if (m == 0 || n == 0 || kTotal == 0) return;\n\n    if (Bpack.empty()) {\n        Bpack.resize(KC * ((NC + NR - 1) / NR) * NR);\n        Apack.resize(((MC + MR - 1) / MR) * MR * KC);\n    }\n    std::vector<double>& Bp = Bpack;\n    std::vector<double>& Ap = Apack;\n\n    for (size_t jc = 0; jc < n; jc += NC) {\n        const size_t nc = std::min(NC, n - jc);\n        for (size_t kc0 = 0; kc0 < kTotal; kc0 += KC) {\n            const size_t kc = std::min(KC, kTotal - kc0);\n            packB(B + kc0 * ldb + jc, ldb, kc, nc, Bp.data());\n\n            for (size_t ic = 0; ic < m; ic += MC) {\n                const size_t mc = std::min(MC, m - ic);\n                packA(A + ic * lda + kc0, lda, mc, kc, Ap.data());\n\n                for (size_t ir = 0; ir < mc; ir += MR) {\n                    const size_t mn = std::min(MR, mc - ir);\n                    const double* aPanel = Ap.data() + (ir / MR) * kc * MR;\n                    for (size_t jr = 0; jr < nc; jr += NR) {\n                        const size_t nn = std::min(NR, nc - jr);\n                        const double* bPanel = Bp.data() + (jr / NR) * kc * NR;\n                        double* cTile = C + (ic + ir) * ldc + jc + jr;\n                        if (mn == MR && nn == NR) {\n                            microKernelFull(aPanel, bPanel, kc, cTile, ldc);\n                        } else {\n                            microKernelEdge(aPanel, bPanel, kc, cTile, ldc, mn, nn);\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n} // namespace\n\n// Simple validation: compute a few elements and compare.\n// Each rank checks the points that fall into the C block it owns; it holds the\n// full A rows and full B columns needed for those, so no communication is required.\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,\n                    const std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N,\n                    const size_t rowBegin, const size_t rowEnd,\n                    const size_t colBegin, const size_t colEnd) {\n    // Check a few random positions\n    constexpr size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};\n\n    const size_t localCols = colEnd - colBegin;\n\n    bool valid = true;\n    for (size_t pi = 0; pi < 5; ++pi) {\n        for (size_t pj = 0; pj < 5; ++pj) {\n            const size_t i = checkPoints[pi] % N;\n            const size_t j = checkPoints[pj] % N;\n\n            if (i < rowBegin || i >= rowEnd || j < colBegin || j >= colEnd) continue;\n            const size_t li = i - rowBegin;\n            const size_t lj = j - colBegin;\n\n            double expected = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {\n                expected += A[li * N + k] * B[k * localCols + lj];\n            }\n\n            const double actual = C[li * localCols + lj];\n            const double relError = std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));\n\n            if (relError > 1e-6) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at (%zu, %zu): expected %.10f, got %.10f (error: %.10e)\\n\",\n                       i, j, expected, actual, relError);\n                valid = false;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    return valid;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Matrix size N (computes NxN * NxN) (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);\n\n    int rank = 0, numRanks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &numRanks);\n\n    size_t N = 512;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            N = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (rank == 0) printUsage(argv[0]);\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Matrix Multiplication Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"Matrix size: %zu x %zu\\n\", N, N);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    }\n\n    // 2D block decomposition of C over a gridRows x gridCols process grid, chosen\n    // as square as possible. Rank r owns the C block of process row r / gridCols and\n    // process column r % gridCols, and correspondingly the A rows and B columns that\n    // block needs. This keeps the per-rank data volume at O(N^2 / sqrt(P)) instead of\n    // the O(N^2) that a pure row decomposition would stream through memory.\n    int gridCols = 1;\n    for (int c = 1; c * c <= numRanks; ++c) {\n        if (numRanks % c == 0) gridCols = c;\n    }\n    const int gridRows = numRanks / gridCols;\n    const int procRow = rank / gridCols;\n    const int procCol = rank % gridCols;\n\n    const auto blockBegin = [](const size_t total, const int parts, const int idx) {\n        const size_t base = total / static_cast<size_t>(parts);\n        const size_t rem = total % static_cast<size_t>(parts);\n        return base * static_cast<size_t>(idx) + std::min(static_cast<size_t>(idx), rem);\n    };\n\n    const size_t rowBegin = blockBegin(N, gridRows, procRow);\n    const size_t rowEnd = blockBegin(N, gridRows, procRow + 1);\n    const size_t colBegin = blockBegin(N, gridCols, procCol);\n    const size_t colEnd = blockBegin(N, gridCols, procCol + 1);\n    const size_t localRows = rowEnd - rowBegin;\n    const size_t localCols = colEnd - colBegin;\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"MPI ranks: %d (process grid %d x %d)\\n\", numRanks, gridRows, gridCols);\n    }\n\n    // Allocate matrices: the A row panel, the B column panel and the C block owned\n    // by this rank.\n    std::vector<double> A(localRows * N);\n    std::vector<double> B(N * localCols);\n    std::vector<double> C(localRows * localCols, 0.0);\n\n    // Initialize matrices\n    if (rank == 0) printf(\"Initializing matrices...\\n\");\n    initMatrixBlock(A, N, rowBegin, rowEnd, 0, N);\n    initMatrixBlock(B, N, 0, N, colBegin, colEnd);\n\n    // Perform matrix multiplication\n    if (rank == 0) printf(\"Computing matrix multiplication...\\n\");\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    localGemm(A.data(), N, B.data(), localCols, C.data(), localCols, localRows, localCols, N);\n\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    long long elapsedMs = duration.count();\n    MPI_Allreduce(MPI_IN_PLACE, &elapsedMs, 1, MPI_LONG_LONG, MPI_MAX, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Computation time: %lld ms\\n\", elapsedMs);\n\n        // Calculate GFLOPS\n        double gflops = (2.0 * N * N * N) / (elapsedMs / 1000.0) / 1e9;\n        printf(\"Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\\n\", gflops);\n    }\n\n    // Print results for external validation: assemble the distributed C blocks\n    // on rank 0 first.\n    if (printResults) {\n        std::vector<double> Cfull;\n        if (rank == 0) {\n            Cfull.resize(N * N);\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < localRows; ++i) {\n                std::copy(C.begin() + i * localCols, C.begin() + (i + 1) * localCols,\n                          Cfull.begin() + (rowBegin + i) * N + colBegin);\n            }\n            for (int r = 1; r < numRanks; ++r) {\n                const size_t rb = blockBegin(N, gridRows, r / gridCols);\n                const size_t re = blockBegin(N, gridRows, r / gridCols + 1);\n                const size_t cb = blockBegin(N, gridCols, r % gridCols);\n                const size_t ce = blockBegin(N, gridCols, r % gridCols + 1);\n                if (re == rb || ce == cb) continue;\n\n                MPI_Datatype blockType;\n                MPI_Type_vector(static_cast<int>(re - rb), static_cast<int>(ce - cb),\n                                static_cast<int>(N), MPI_DOUBLE, &blockType);\n                MPI_Type_commit(&blockType);\n                MPI_Recv(Cfull.data() + rb * N + cb, 1, blockType, r, 1, MPI_COMM_WORLD,\n                         MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);\n                MPI_Type_free(&blockType);\n            }\n        } else if (localRows > 0 && localCols > 0) {\n            MPI_Send(C.data(), static_cast<int>(localRows * localCols), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, 1,\n                     MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n        }\n\n        if (rank == 0) print_results(Cfull, \"MatrixC\");\n    }\n\n    // Validation\n    int rc = 0;\n    if (validate) {\n        if (rank == 0) printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n        int valid = validateResult(A, B, C, N, rowBegin, rowEnd, colBegin, colEnd) ? 1 : 0;\n        MPI_Allreduce(MPI_IN_PLACE, &valid, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_LAND, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n        if (valid) {\n            if (rank == 0) printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            rc = 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            rc = 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    MPI_Finalize();\n    return rc;\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":30,"oldLines":8,"newStart":30,"newLines":8,"lines":[" }"," "," // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------","-// Node-local GEMM: cache-blocked, packed, register-blocked kernel.","-// Computes C(m x N) += A(m x N) * B(N x N) for the locally owned row block.","+// Rank-local GEMM: cache-blocked, packed, register-blocked kernel computing the","+// C block owned by this rank."," // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------"," "," namespace {"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CeezGgYs4ksUnW8DZ6bms","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Done. `matmul/matmul.cpp` and `matmul/CMakeLists.txt` are the only files changed; the executable is still `matmul`.\n\n**What it does now**\n\n1. **2D block decomposition (MPI).** Ranks form a `gridRows × gridCols` grid as square as possible. Rank *r* owns the C block `[rowBegin,rowEnd) × [colBegin,colEnd)`, and stores only the A row panel and B column panel that block needs — O(N²/√P) memory per rank instead of O(N²). Since `getPseudoRndValue` is a pure function of `(N,i,j)`, each rank generates exactly its own input slices during initialization, so the multiply phase needs no communication at all (the alternative I benchmarked — a 1D row split with an overlapped ring exchange of B blocks — reached only 142 GFLOPS at 128 ranks vs 1617 for the 2D layout, because the skinny row blocks are memory-bandwidth bound and the P-step ring adds sync overhead).\n2. **Rank-local GEMM rewritten** as a packed, cache-blocked kernel (MC/KC/NC = 120/256/512) with a 6×2-vector register-blocked micro-kernel written in portable GCC/Clang vector extensions (AVX-512/AVX/SSE2/NEON widths selected at compile time; no new dependencies). Single-core: 16.7 GFLOPS vs ~2.7 for the original loop, ≈65% of single-threaded OpenBLAS on this machine.\n3. **MPI in the rest of the program:** barrier-bracketed timing with a max-reduce over ranks, rank-0-only console output, `-r` assembles C on rank 0 via `MPI_Type_vector` block receives, `-v` validates check points locally on the owning rank (it holds the full A row and B column) with an `MPI_LAND` reduce, and all exit paths call `MPI_Finalize`.\n\n**Measured (EPYC 7763, 2×64 cores, N=4096):** 16.7 / 63.8 / 253 / 892 / 1617 GFLOPS at 1 / 4 / 16 / 64 / 128 ranks — 97× on 128 cores. N=8192 at 128 ranks: 2051 GFLOPS.\n\n**Correctness:** validated at many rank counts and sizes (N = 7, 300, 512, 517, 1024, 2048…, including P > N and prime P). Results are bit-identical regardless of rank count, and bit-identical to the original serial program for power-of-two N. Two caveats worth stating: for non-power-of-two N the packed kernel's k-blocking plus FMA contraction changes the last bits versus the original serial summation order (`Sum`, `Min`, `Max` and samples agree to all 17 printed digits; the `-v` check passes well inside its 1e-6 tolerance) — forcing bit-exactness there requires an unblocked k loop and `-ffp-contract=off`, which costs 2.6–5× at scale. And when ranks don't factor well (e.g. 127) the grid degenerates to 1D and throughput drops roughly 3×."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":608,"cache_read_input_tokens":115499,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":608},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"b347bdc0-37bb-4c4e-9eb7-c2215c9db0e3","uuid":"62eb52c2-7544-4592-aee5-089f6e543c6b","timestamp":"2026-09-02T17:14:14.186Z","request_id":"req_011CeezGfweYSj6KFiC4TDgH"}
